# Changelog

## Unreleased
### Highlights

- **Sandboxing and container isolation.** `sampo sandbox run -- CMD` runs a command in its own user, mount, pid, ipc and uts namespaces through bubblewrap. The system is read-only, and only the project and `--write` folders are writable. Credential folders and files (SSH and GPG keys, cloud and GitHub logins, Sampo's secrets, browser profiles) read as empty, secret-looking variables are dropped, and there is no session bus or docker socket. The network is off, on, or `--net allow`: only listed hosts, through a filtering proxy, never cloud metadata addresses, and loopback only by name with its port. `sampo sandbox status` and `sampo doctor` name each layer and whether it is live. `sampo sandbox on --session` turns on Claude Code's own sandbox for your sessions, with ssh-based git and `sampo ship` left outside it, and `off --session` puts settings.json back. `run.py --sandbox` runs each test class with the repo read-only and no network. Nothing is installed, and without bubblewrap everything runs as before (T-3222). A test class whose temp folder can't be made (a /tmp out of space or inodes) now fails by name instead of crashing the whole test run (T-3236).
- **Foreman is now Sampo.** The CLI is `sampo`, and its parts carry their Kalevala names: Smith (the task loop), Rune (the tower), Moons (the idea works), Louhi (the exam), Underworld (what isn't possible yet) and Ward (the guard). Nothing you have breaks. `fm`, every old verb, `foreman@foreman`, `/foreman:` commands, `foreman:` agents, `FOREMAN_*` variables and `Foreman-Task` trailers keep working as aliases. `~/.claude/foreman` moves once, with a backup and a symlink at the old path, and `sampo migrate --undo` restores it byte for byte.
- **A new look: the illustrated forge.** The anvil-stone logo, the README header, the site, the tower pages, the pane and the band share one design system (`design/sampo/tokens.json`), in light and dark.
- **Local models do real work.** Sampo serves Ollama and other OpenAI-style endpoints with the context and reasoning settings each model needs, runs its own loop on them in Louhi's exam, and grows playbooks written for 4B to 27B models. On the first exam, a 12B model went from 0 to 3 of 8 scenarios fixed once it was served 32k of context with reasoning off.
- **Moons at scale.** `sampo moons grow` fills a grid of 10,000 candidate moons, and a cheap-first funnel (dedupe, kill test, tier, novelty) and families cut them down to the few worth a moon page.

### Everything else

- Conductor send and steer put the remote text after --, so a message that starts with a dash is no longer read as an option by the remote bus (T-3955).
- Small-model mode works where it is advertised: a model profile's "small": true now turns it on (a non-boolean is refused by name), an exam row and line say "small" and name the planner, so a --small arm and a plain one of the same model read apart, and sampo code --resume of a --small run stays small and remembers an edit made before the crash, instead of refusing its finish as early do… (T-3974).
- a close waits up to 20 s for full gates whose last timed run was that quick and prints green or red, so a small repo's session reports the result instead of 'still running'; an S fix close no longer nudges for --why-not-caught, and a request clause that only restates the title is no longer listed as meaning unproven (T-4015).
- Sampo's refusals, warnings and notices now start with sampo: instead of the old fm: name, and a retry hint says Next: sampo …; the readers of those lines accept both (T-4020).
- the SAMPO panel counts a sampo command as failed when its output is a refusal, even when a pipe such as | tail hid the exit code (T-4021).
- a merged lane's close is judged on what its train merge brought to the build branch, not on the build branch's changes the lane merged into itself, so hand closes stop citing other tasks' files as security-sensitive or out of scope (T-4026).
- sampo lane merge T-A T-B T-C and lane merge --all land several finished lanes as one train, each lane's checks and probe tests running in its own process (no shared GIL), with the self-change gate's timing bench behind a machine-wide lock (T-3119).
- The merge train no longer stops on a hook-speed check that only looks red: the hook bench now also counts the machine as busy when CPU pressure (the share of time tasks wait for a CPU) is 10% or more, since the 1-minute load average lags behind it, and a train stopped on a red base now shows the red check's own lines, such as the bench's OVER BUDGET rows (T-4029).
- Merge trains, lane merges, ship and sampo check no longer wait 60 s for a machine that has been busy for 5 minutes: they go ahead at once with the longer timeout, and only a short spike is waited out (T-3961).
- `sampo doctor` now checks the recordings in `demo/` and `docs/assets/screens/`. It fails when one shows a retired name (`fm`, Foreman, a renamed verb such as `sampo code`), warns when one is over 21 days old with its code changed since, and names the command that remakes it; Sampo's scout files either as work. `sampo demo` now prints `sampo` commands, and its recording, the Sampo Code video and the tour SVG are re-recorded from today's code (T-3989).
- the hook latency bench counts load against the cores the session's CPU cap leaves (cgroup cpu.max), so a capped session's throttled wall time no longer turns the land's base red (T-4018).
- Ward: a brief refusal whose write target is in the project only because a cd may have failed now names that cd and says to chain it with && (T-3713).
- the README's Claude Code demo and still are re-recorded from the Board pane's ten panels; the pane's merge panel names the main checkout's branch, and a sampo command shows in its SAMPO panel on the next poll instead of up to 30 s later (T-4008).
- A merge train no longer throws its green work away when the main checkout has uncommitted changes to files it merged: it sets them aside, fast-forwards and puts them back, keeping one that no longer applies as a named stash with the landed files in place; and a close now commits the CHANGELOG line it writes, which it used to leave uncommitted whenever another lane's merge had changed the file (T-4007).
- The live page's POST /api/act now refuses any request a proxy has marked Forwarded or X-Forwarded-*, and one test attacks each act (drive, checkpoint, pause, note) with no token, a stale token, another Origin, a forwarded Host or port and proxy headers, then runs it for the user (T-3063).
- The land closes each merged task and ships with a fresh sampo process, so verbs and docs merged during a long land run no longer get its closes refused as unknown commands (T-3970).
- An urgent task gets a builder lane even while 20 finished lanes wait on a merge, so the fixes that drain a merge backlog are never held behind it; every other task still waits (T-3964).
- `sampo conductor send NAME "text"` messages one added remote or one live session by its id prefix, not all of them; an unknown name exits 1 listing the known names (T-3932).
- sampo lane accept ID records the review audits you give, checks the close fields and evidence gaps, and queues the lane first in the running land (or starts one detached); sampo lane recover --all takes back every stalled lane; sampo do NAME runs a saved routine of up to three verbs from plugin/macros (sampo do --list) (T-3922).
- `sampo repeats --sequences` mines routines of 3–6 tool calls that sessions repeat from transcripts and the event log, ranked by calls saved, each with an example (T-3921).
- Close fields are read from a builder's report even when a Noticed: line follows the close block (T-3920).
- A task closed inside its own lane whose branch holds unmerged commits now counts as a finished lane, so sampo lane land and --first train it (T-3907).
- Usage horizon: sampo budget set --until paces optional work by the recent weekly burn rate against the allowance to that time, not the fixed 75% floor; sampo budget shows used %, rate, allowance and projection (T-3902).
- A land remembers each refused close with its reason, brief hash and lane tip, retries it when either changes or after 6 h, closes merged leftovers after the trains, and counts the skipped retries (T-3884).
- A lane lands without the 30-minute quiet wait once its builder has returned, or when sampo lane land --first names it, instead of every finished lane sitting half an hour after its last commit (T-3824).
- install.sh passes shellcheck again, so fm prove --infra stops failing (a newly installed shellcheck flagged SC2155 at the uv Python step), and a uv that can't name its Python folder stops the install with a message (T-3822).
- Inherited test classes no longer rerun their base's tests, and a hygiene test fails on an unmarked rerun (T-3815).
- sampo task prove keeps code in a tests folder (run.py, forkcli.py) at its start version, so a runner change's red-to-green proof is no longer passed by a copy (T-3808).
- The post-merge verify no longer falls back to one full suite per landed merge when its one run on main's HEAD goes red: the red classes rerun alone at HEAD (green: a flake, and the HEAD run covers every pending merge), or else alone on the pending merges to the first one they fail on; the merges before it are covered, and only that merge gets its own full verify (T-3800).
- The Ward no longer reads a command stored in a variable on the same line (R="sampo task dissent T-1 resolve"; $R 6 …) as every dangerous command it could be: a value set once to a literal is checked as that value; an unknown one (X=$(cat f); $X 6) still is (T-3795).
- Piping a sampo check command that runs no gate (check add, rm, list, paths, affected, onwrite and the like, or --help) is no longer refused; a piped gate run (sampo check, check run, --affected) still is, and an unknown subcommand stays refused (T-3776).
- Merge trains go gate-first: when the train's gate is the affected test suite, no lane's tests run first in a probe of their own; the gate runs them once on the merged tree, a red gate still names its lane by bisection, and FOREMAN_TRAIN_PROBES=1 brings the probes back (T-3770).
- Performance stays fixed: merge trains hold every test class to its usual CPU (the median of its last green runs, counting the processes it orphans and its forked fm calls), so a lane that makes a class twice as costly (and 20 CPU s more), adds a new class over 90 CPU s without a declared budget, or raises the run's known classes 1.5x together is left out like a red test, with the number… (T-3757).
- The risk budget has an off switch only the user can set: /fm-risk off|on [here] in the Claude Code mod (typed by the user and written straight to disk, never by a tool call, so an agent still can't lift its own brake), globally or for one project; and a guard block the agent rewrites around no longer spends a point (T-3752).
- `sampo bench swe` runs SWE-bench Verified instances in their official docker images under two harnesses, the bare model and Smith, and `sampo bench runs` shows them side by side with the delta and failure classes (T-3746).
- The live Board gains an All mode that draws every project's lanes, merges and loops at once, colour-coded with filter chips, from a per-project cache (T-3730).
- Affected-test selection ignores a class's old-format run-cache records when it has no newer one: T-3603 fell back to them, and since they name every module a class imported as read whole, nearly every change selected all ~416 test modules until a full re-record; only function-keyed records add tests now (T-3711).
- `sampo check --affected` in a lane that merged the main branch now picks tests only for the lane's own changes, not for every file the main branch changed meanwhile, so the run is much smaller (T-3696).
- The short profile summary a new session or subagent gets now lists every standing rule in whole words (autonomy, privacy, GitHub and more) instead of cutting four items off mid-sentence (T-3690).
- The big-file read outline test now checks the short packed outline (names several per line, at most 8 lines), so the build stays green after the outline was shortened (T-3679).
- the test suite uses less CPU: seeded test classes copy a ready-made git repo, fm code runs in tests go through the fork server, and the fork server no longer spawns a manager process per call, cutting a profiled 823-test sample by about 13% (T-3678).
- the local-model (Smith) exam now grounds each finding in the code, keeps only the most serious one and states a confidence, which cut false alarms from 12 to 2 and raised found defects from 6 to 12 of 24 on a held-out run (T-3676).
- when the guard refuses a curl piped into python3 -c only because other commands on the same line write files, the message now tells you to run that pipeline as its own command instead of leaving you to ask for a pipe-shell grant (T-3673).
- sampo's task new and capture start faster because the verb help index is kept in the package cache folder instead of rebuilding the whole command parser on every call, cutting a profiled test-run sample from 431 to 331 CPU-seconds (T-3667).
- sampo ship now pushes the newest commit the post-merge verify already passed in full and reuses that run, so it no longer re-runs the whole suite (about 80 CPU-minutes) on a HEAD that moved on; --head ships HEAD as before (T-3666).
- A task briefed for a builder that never started (no lane 30 minutes after its brief) no longer holds a builder slot; three such briefs held the cap of 2 for two days and refused every builder (T-3995).
- The test run cache now records which functions each test class actually ran, so editing one function in a big file like core.py reruns only the classes that ran that function instead of nearly the whole suite (T-3603).
- The live tower now pushes to main only when something really changed (not just timestamps, commit ids or counters) and at most once an hour, so it no longer floods main with a Pages deploy per update (T-3504).
- Smith's harness now runs the failing-frame and outline cards before the first turn and applies a matching fix card with the model filling only its open slot, so a local model no longer has to choose to call a card (T-3181).
- `sampo task finish --outcome` records a typed signal that `sampo outcomes --silent`, `sampo next` and `sampo doctor` probe at 1, 7 and 30 days, and `sampo outcomes --census` lists the never-used and ineffective verbs, skills, playbooks, night jobs and hook notes (T-3934).
- A lane fixed while its train was still running is no longer parked for good: a park now records the commit the land tested, and a new commit on the lane lifts it (the sampo-ui mod waited 14 hours behind one such park) (T-3994).
- The site is one site: every page (the landing, the tower, Cards, each feature page and case study) carries the landing's header, with six links instead of eleven, a Sections menu on small screens, the Daylight/Forge switch and GitHub, and loads the site's fonts (feature pages fell back to system fonts). The Features index is grouped into seven numbered stations in the landing's type; the side list follows the same groups and opens at the page you are on. New Kalevala page; the Where you see it page shows the Board, the sampo-ui pane and Smith as they are now, and Builder lanes shows the merge train. A test now fails when a page drifts from the landing's header or fonts, or when a README feature has no page (T-3996).
- A private name (`sampo private`) or a secrets-file value is now caught when a lane merges, naming the commit and file, so it is amended in its lane; before, only `sampo ship` caught it, after later work had built on it, and every ship was refused until history was rewritten. When only earlier commits held it, `sampo ship` now sends the range to main as one commit with the clean tree. A test that named a real remote address now uses a documentation address (T-3999).
- The Ward no longer asks for the credentials grant to edit or delete a UI source file named for design tokens, such as a gallery page Tokens.svelte or tokens.css; tokens.json and token files outside design and web folders still count as credentials (T-3908).
- A lane that has merged and waits only for its close no longer counts as an unmerged lane or a busy builder, so a backlog of hand closes stops refusing every new builder (22 did on 2026-11-08) (T-3847).
- The user's direction leads the profile every agent starts with: a user memory marked lead: true comes before the seed lines, so the session and subagent digests, which keep only about the first eight items, always carry it (T-3846).
- Kalevala's towers climb past their first step: a tower's spec reads the root's exam run instead of writing one (which a tower may not do, so all 26 towers had failed it since 2026-11-06), a failed step keeps its reason and sampo kalevala status shows it, and a round where every tower fails becomes a FIX task (T-3841).
- sampo task ac edit corrects the words of a criterion that already has evidence when its verify command stays the same (logged), so a brief can say what was built; a changed check is still refused (T-3823).
- The merge train's hook bench judges a busy machine by each hook's CPU median instead of its p95, which load inflates (one commit measured 404 then 106 ms), so a load spike no longer leaves a good lane out while a slower hook still fails (T-3821).
- sampo lane land names, before its first train, each lane whose close will be refused for a missing line (DOCS, NUMBERS, LESSON, why not caught), so the line can be added while the gates run, and each hand close's progress line says why, not only the final report hours later (T-3819).
- The Ward no longer refuses a design-tokens file in a project's web asset folder (plugin/web/live/board-tokens.json, static/css/tokens.json) as a credential; tokens.json at a repo root, under a dot folder or anywhere in ~/.config still is one (T-3810).
- The Ward no longer refuses a line just because a double-quoted $( ) sits on it: the substitution is read as code with its own quoted text masked, so a quoted grep pattern like "|let users" beside sed -n "$(grep …)p" is data, and a read's here-string or < file (IFS=: read n port <<< "$a") is its input, not a name it sets; a substitution holding a case pattern, heredoc or comment still ke… (T-3799).
- `sampo prove --infra` checks a compose override (docker-compose.gpu.yml) together with the base file beside it, as compose runs them, and calls a compose file unproven, not failed, when it reads variables from an .env the copy leaves out; both had failed a valid stack and held every close on a project with a GPU override (T-3897)
- The plugin is now sampo@sampo (with sampo-ui@sampo and sampo-design@sampo) in a marketplace named sampo; it was still foreman@foreman. sampo migrate plugins moves an existing install in one write per file, so the old and new ids are never enabled together, and --undo restores the files byte for byte. install.sh runs it for you, sampo doctor says when it's due, and /foreman: commands bec… (T-3707).
- `sampo readme` gives any project's README real figures: `plan` proposes them from the repo, `shots` screenshots the running app at desktop and phone in light and dark (a page showing private text is refused), `diagram` draws dark and light SVGs from a spec in the project's own colours or the Board look, and `check` holds every image to its file, alt text, pair and size; the `readme-visu… (T-3703).
- The Ward no longer trusts a program given as a path by its name alone: ./python3 or /tmp/x/python3 counts as Python 3 (or a data tool) only when the file it really runs, links followed, is one only root can change, so a copy of bash named python3 no longer passes the downloaded-data exception; a venv's bin/python over the system python still does (T-3693).
- Every Sampo call now spends less CPU: timestamp parsing, revert history, brief search words, the guard's pattern setup and `sampo --version` no longer redo work that has not changed, which cuts a forked focus from about 112 ms to 50 ms on a 402-brief project (T-3692).
- The run cache keys a class's reads of the repo's branch, a remote's default branch or a file at HEAD instead of the whole tree, and judges a driven browser by the pages it is sent. The recorder starts once, judges each stat once and writes each line once, so the guard runs +6–15% slower under it instead of +72–84% (T-3691).
- Ward now treats only python3 and python3.N as safe for `curl … | python -c` data pipes, so python2 (whose input() runs piped text as code) and an ambiguous plain python stay refused (T-3689).
- A message from another Claude session (or agent, reminder or notification) is no longer read as something you typed, so it can't be recorded as a request, steer or approval; your own pasted HTML still counts (T-3687).
- When Ward refuses a command because it could not parse the quoting (for example text like "at 1:23" inside a node -e string), the message now says so and tells you to use a heredoc or a file before it offers any grant (T-3684).
- Ward no longer blocks `curl … | python3 -I -c CODE` (or -E, -IB and other flags that only narrow what python reads) or the same pipeline inside a for/while polling loop, while -i, -m and any writer in the loop still stay refused (T-3683).
- fm's per-call overhead is cut: forked fm calls were 48% of the test suite's CPU, and every call paid for imports and parsers it never used; --version builds no parser (T-3677).
- every agent on the machine now starts knowing the user's standing preferences: a new sampo profile verb builds a scrubbed, size-capped machine-local profile from the cross-project memories and hands it to Claude Code sessions in any folder, subagents, builder briefs, sampo code agents and MCP clients, without writing it to any repo or CLAUDE.md (T-3672).
- A project checked out as a git worktree under a system folder such as /opt (its .git is a file, not a folder) is now treated as a checkout, so the Ward no longer refuses edits in its lanes as writes to the system (T-3890).
- Panel kit: `sampo panels render` draws 13 mechanism panels (cluster, funnel, transform, globe, lanes, filetree, terminal, tanks, orbit, coremap, river, gate, arch) as static SVGs in board, blueprint and paper styles, each a typed calling card (T-3662).
- SessionStart restores crash-emptied files once per boot instead of listing and statting every tracked file at each session start, so a session start costs ~40% less (T-3820).
- The Ward keeps a variable set once on the line (S=<scratch folder>) known when a later word only spells its name as a value (tier=S, --kind=S) or inside quoted text (a sed script's 'S = 2'), so a scratch write through $S/… is no longer refused as a project edit; a quoted name a builtin really sets (read "S", declare "S=…") still makes it unknown (T-3803).
- fm doctor times hooks only on a calm machine (under 0.5 load per CPU, the SLO row's own bar), so a merge train's doctor gate no longer fails on hook p95 timings a loaded machine inflates (it left two good lanes out on 2026-11-08); and test_doctor stops running Checks' 36 tests a second time under PermissionMode (T-3814).
- A criterion's verify warning (run.py names no test, -k matches none, a pipe hides the exit status) now ends the command's own result line too, from sampo task new --ac (after --focus as well), task ac add and task ac edit, so a caller reading only the last line sees it (T-3813).
- The risk budget forgives a guard block only when it was a likely false refusal: an rm outside the project, a destructive git call or a piped shell, rewritten with the same program; credentials, system, self-authorize and confine blocks always cost their point, and a session is forgiven at most three times a day, so an agent can't clear its points by following each blocked attempt with a… (T-3812).
- The Ward's downloaded-data exception (curl … | python3 -c reading json from stdin) now allows sed that only prints (sed -n '…p', s/// with only g, p, I flags; no -i, -f, w or e) and docker's read-only verbs (port, ps, inspect, logs, images, version, info, top), or docker exec running such a tool, elsewhere on the line; docker cp, run, compose and exec of anything else still make it refu… (T-3807).
- sampo task set ID --section A --file a --section B --file b fills both sections, each from its own file, as its help said; only the last --file was kept before (T-3805).
- The Ward no longer confines the main session as a builder when it has cd'd into a subagent builder's worktree (.claude/worktrees/agent-<id>) to run its tests or merge main in: there only the subagent's own calls, which carry an agent_id, are confined; lanes made by sampo lane new stay confined as before (T-3802).
- sampo lane land trains urgent lanes first, then performance and security fixes, then the user's own asks, then the rest (oldest first in each); sampo lane land --first T-1,T-2 puts lanes ahead, queued into a running land at its next train boundary; and each train's lanes close as soon as it lands them (lanes an earlier train merged close before the first train), so a long land no longer… (T-3796).
- The plugin is now `sampo@sampo` (with `sampo-ui@sampo` and `sampo-design@sampo`) in a marketplace named `sampo`; it was still `foreman@foreman`. `sampo migrate plugins` moves an existing install in one write per file, so the old and new ids are never enabled together, and `--undo` restores the files byte for byte. install.sh runs it for you, and `sampo doctor` says when it's due. `/foreman:` commands become `/sampo:` (T-3707, T-2879)
- The coverage recorder no longer counts sleep, nvidia-smi and similar programs run from the repo as reading the whole tree, reads --opt=path, file:// URLs with query or escapes as the paths they name, and keeps DevTools-driven browsers blind, so 3 more costly classes can be skipped when unchanged (T-3623)
- Single-kind ledger readers (cost, night, outcomes, contract, graph, recall, ideas, rune, grants, mcp, playbooks, cli, hooks) decode only their kind's lines, about 3.7x less CPU per read on a 50k-line ledger (T-3586)
- The Sampo pane and band show the work in lanes (building, waiting to merge, close owed), the whole queue's count instead of the list's cap of 20, and the user's own asks first in the inbox with how many are theirs (T-3664)
- The post-merge verify skips classes green at the same code through the coverage cache (opt-in SAMPO_CACHE_FULL, stripped from child envs, 1-in-10 resample kept; every 6th verify or 4 h runs uncached), while ship stays one truly full run (T-3638)
- A merge train's report line gives the minutes its probe, gate and land phases took and how many probes a held green run skipped, so the slow phase of a merge is visible (T-3596)
- Every regen_views call made under the project lock now exports the mirror there and builds the views after the lock is released, so about 50 more commands hold the lock for their writes only (lock body 0.08-0.14 s -> 0.006 s) (T-3613)
- The self-change gate judges a lane merged onto the base, as the train lands it, instead of its tip, so a lane built before a guard-tightening commit is no longer held for loosening it (T-3647)
- One table of speed promises for the hot CLI verbs, measured on child CPU time as a ratio to bare python, with hook budgets from bench_hooks (T-3272).
- The hook bench reads the script budget from a -I child and clamps it, so a branch's guard code can't patch or loosen the clock that times it (T-3132).
- The UI card grid is grown: 12,000 UI cards across 2,541 of its 3,840 cells (66%), written by Haiku and the local models; cards name each platform's own test runner, card commands are checked the way bash reads them, and the grower runs repairs in parallel and stops cleanly when interrupted (T-2994)
- Test classes no longer inherit operator switches (FOREMAN_LOAD_GATE, SAMPO_SLOT and the like), a test makes every new env name Sampo reads declare itself switch or harness, and a merge train checks the base's affected tests first whenever its env differs from the last green train's, so an env change that turns the base red stops it in minutes (T-3625)
- Page tests (live page and four site modules) share one headless browser per run with a fresh context per test (69% less launch CPU over 20 empty pages, ABAB), night generator jobs run at nice 19 and other night jobs at nice 10 at most (T-3296)
- `sampo cpu` shows this session's CPU from its cgroup as cores and % of the machine, split by kind, a self-check says how far the 10-minute average is over the 10% goal and what uses most, and `sampo cpu cap` sets an optional quota on the session's own scope (T-3592).
- \`sampo docs --trace\` links each doc claim to the code lines that back it, definitions first; \`--since REF\` marks claims whose backing changed and \`--strict\` fails on an unbacked one (T-3287).
- \`sampo lane brief --ready\` briefs every ready S/M task in one call and prints one block of launch lines, with \`--json\` for parallel Agent calls; a batch stops at the first refusal that holds for all, never briefs two tasks sharing a file, and caps its skip list (T-3293).
- README diagrams (the loop, the Ward, how the pieces fit) redrawn in Sampo's modern look and name as dark and light pairs, each passing \`sampo works check\` (T-3248).
- run.py now skips a test class when none of the repo files it read when it last passed have changed, recorded across every Python process it starts and shared by every worktree of the repo, so trains, closes and builders rerun only what a change can reach (T-3593)
- run.py skips a test class already green at the same content of its test file, imported helpers, the plugin tree and the files the map links to it, reruns 1 in 10 of them and drops the cache when one fails; the full gate, -k runs and FOREMAN_NO_TEST_CACHE=1 never skip; a class is recorded only when its key is unchanged after the run and none of its tests skipped (T-3502).
- A red merge train names its culprit lanes from its own failure output (the file a failure names, the lane that changed the failing test, the lane whose diff reaches it) and bisects only an ambiguous red, each probe the failing classes alone once they're shown to fail alone, then confirms with the other gates and only those classes plus the left-out lanes' reach (T-3602)
- `sampo next`, `fm ui`, and the Stop and UserPromptSubmit hooks stop re-reading every brief and the whole ledger: a self-checking index of event kinds and of open briefs means each call reads only what changed (T-3573).
- The self-change gate's planted-growth proofs (LeanKindGate*) bench 4 hook fixtures in-process and skip the import-map run: about 2.5x faster under load, same refusals (T-3379).
- fm sync import no longer turns a task that is active in the main checkout or a lane back to planned when the repo's mirror copy is an older runnable one; blocked, deferred and closed repo copies still win (T-3396).
- Every hook keeps its compiled regex patterns between calls, so a PreToolUse Bash call no longer recompiles about 120 of them (CPU 53.8 -> 45.5 ms per call) (T-3584)
- Timing tests hold under load (interleaved A/B, event waits, load-scaled deadlines), the exam's planted hook wait is 3x the hook process's own age so the self-change gate sees it at any load, and a merge train re-runs a red acceptance command once and notes it flaky instead of leaving the lane out (T-3629)
- The Ward treats anything that could swap or poison `plugin/native/sampo-fast` as a protected-core write: cargo, rustc or rustup output aimed into `plugin/`, build settings or a toolchain path on a main `plugin/native` build, cargo config and toolchain files at or above it, cargo's `bin/` and rustup's toolchains, and any `.cargo/config` with a path that resolves into the main checkout; `--config FILE` and rustc `@FILE` are refused unread (T-3607)
- The tests' scratch repo is copied from a per-process template and only `git commit` runs per test, cutting git processes from 5 to 1 per repo and fixture CPU from 8.4 to 4.0 ms (T-3581)
- One shared nav for the landing, the tower and the section pages (a single NAV list in site/build.py, with a menu on narrow screens), so Install and GitHub are back on the tower and the links can't drift again (T-2903).
- A builder's start/stop mark now survives a busy project lock: the brief is found before the lock, one brief is re-read and saved under it with retries, and contention is one counted hooks.log line instead of a traceback (T-3609)
- `sampo --version` and `sampo version` print Sampo's version, and the verbs agents most often guess (`models`, `where`, `task scope`, `lane awaiting`, `affected`, `task approve`) now name the right one instead of a misspelling match (T-3612)
- The Ward now reads a sampo verb, gated word or stored command the shell fills in (a variable, a substitution, xargs input, an alias, a nameref or anything else that rebinds a name, env -S, source <(...), a computed path to sampo) through the values the line sets only where nothing in the shell can rebind them, refuses one it still can't read (no grant lifts it), and says whether to type the verb or write the value into the command (T-3600)
- The prompt and session-start hooks, `sampo capture` and `task new` hold the project lock only for their writes, with the brief load, state build, view regeneration and rewind git snapshot moved after or before it (T-3582)
- One load signal for timeouts: core.load_factor (max of the 1-minute load and the runnable count, per CPU, with the instant runnable term capped at 4x) now scales gate timeouts, run.py's class timers and the tests' hang guards alike, and FOREMAN_HOST accepts runnable= (T-3391).
- fm doctor's hook SLO judges only PreToolUse runs with a recorded load under 0.5 per CPU (T-2798's 1.5 bar let busy-machine runs in); with fewer than 20 calm runs, or none in the last 24 h, it reports "not enough (recent) calm samples" and passes, and shows the oldest calm sample's age (T-2933).
- Session liveness readers (_other_session and the focus note) also scan the per-session sessions/<sid>.seen markers, so a second session working autonomously no longer looks idle; markers carry the Claude pid and project, so a resume, /clear or a session in another project is not mistaken for another session here; workers and lane runners write none, and markers over a day old are pruned (T-3422).
- Session liveness refreshes from tool calls too: PostToolUse moves the session's seen stamp at most once a minute, gated by a per-session marker file's mtime so most calls cost one stat; a busy lock skips the refresh without waiting, so a session doing long autonomous work no longer looks dead to the second-session lane gate (T-3389).
- `sampo doctor` warns once, naming each MCP server configured with the deprecated HTTP+SSE transport (`"type": "sse"`, in ~/.claude.json, per project and the project's .mcp.json), and warns when either file can't be read; cites the MCP 2026-07-28 changelog (T-3346).
- Local models take turns on the shared GPU instead of evicting each other on every call, the machine's Ollama is no longer polled twice a second for its model list, and a model Ollama can't load is asked once and then skipped for 6 hours (T-3597, T-3598, T-3599).
- Test runs share one warm fork server across all their classes and send hook calls through it too, so a test's sampo and hook calls no longer start a fresh interpreter, and the forked calls' CPU now shows up in a run's timings (T-3572).
- The merge train's gate and probes, `sampo ship`'s suite and `sampo check` in the main checkout now take freed CPU test slots ahead of builder lanes' runs, so a gate no longer gets 1/N of the pool beside ~10 testing builders (T-3614)
- Local Ollama models that sit fully on the GPU now run with one CPU thread through Ollama's native chat API, because llama.cpp's eight default threads only busy-wait there and the OpenAI endpoint can't pass the setting (T-3588).
- The genesis tower's live publish now runs the same private-term and secrets-value scan as sampo ship on the files its commit touches, and records a refusal instead of pushing; its export redacts any home path, private IP and private term in both keys and values (T-3551).
- finished_lanes reads every lane's tip in one worktree list and one log walk, and keeps each lane's answer until its tip, index or main moves, so a refresh with unchanged lanes runs 2 git processes instead of ~260 (T-3585).
- Hooks no longer log a traceback when the project lock is busy: skips are counted per event in one friction line (sampo doctor warns when a hold passes 60 s or the count passes 50), a lock timeout names the last known holder's pid, command and seconds since it took the lock (redacted), lock waits back off to 100 ms polls, builder marks stop taking the lock unless a lane matches, and the Stop and SessionStart writes retry once (T-3535).
- A close whose reuse of the gates' pass misses now says why in one line (the files that differ from the newest pass, or no pass), and a self-check names untracked, non-ignored files in the main checkout that keep every close off the train's pass (T-3548).
- PreToolUse calls Sampo can prove it allows (Read, Agent/Task, outward reads) can now be answered by a checksum-verified native Rust binary in about 4 ms of CPU instead of 30-40 ms of Python; every other call, and every machine without a verified binary, runs the Python hook unchanged (T-3571)
- sampo lane land trains every finished lane in batches, closes each landed task from the builder's report lines (CHANGELOG, TITLE, LESSON, NUMBERS, DOCS, recorded on its brief at the builder's return and read by sampo task finish), parks the rest with the reason, and ships on a publish grant already given; the drive names it for 2+ waiting lanes (T-3500).
- A brief refusal for a path inside a builder lane or a .claude/worktrees worktree now names that lane, its task and the exact quoted `cd <lane> && sampo focus <ID>`, instead of telling the agent to start a new task in the main checkout (T-3534).
- A git merge of the build branch typed in a lane's own worktree no longer counts as an edit of the project, as long as git is the first word and nothing on the line sets a GIT_* variable; a merge refused because the cd may not hold now names that cd instead of sampo lane merge (T-3537, T-3553).
- `sampo task finish` refuses an edit outside the task's scope before any check runs, and a file that was untracked at the task's start and removed since is no longer counted as the task's edit; the close names it in a warning instead (T-3550).
- The Sampo pane reruns fm ui at most every 10 s for busy files, and at once when a pause, trust, an ask, drive or the active task changes; each run keeps git's answers until a ref moves and reads the ledgers from where it stopped, cutting fm ui from ~1,100 to ~110 CPU-s/h (T-3580).
- `fm ui --json` keeps its JSON in the project's cache/ while HEAD, meta.json, the ledger, the tasks/ folder, trust and the pause flag stand still (at most 10 s, and the pane asks again after a cached reply when its paths moved), so the pane's poll after every tool call costs ~0.2 s CPU instead of ~0.8 s, and its kept band lines no longer wake the pane (T-3542).
- risk.json, genesis-step.json and doctor-cache.json no longer get rewritten by lanes and main for each other: risk keeps one HEAD-reflog entry per checkout under the worktree's real git dir, and rune.health and doctor key on content hashes (c.content_key), not on mtimes or the plugin path (T-3565).
- sampo focus now refuses an unplanned, lane-only or held task before its git snapshot, recall and predict, and the views still update when saving the list of the user's uncommitted files fails (T-3583)
- The test runner no longer crashes on a file whose name isn't valid UTF-8: the coverage cache reads git's output, hashes and saves such paths with surrogate escapes (a crash failed the 05:20 ship's full suite) (T-3651)
- A close after a green merge train reuses the train's pass: its linked tests are counted the way the train picks them (another runner's tests only when a gate runs that runner; sync-mirror .md files no longer link tests), and a newer run on other code no longer hides the pass on this code (T-3566).
- Hooks no longer rebuild the whole task state for the taint check, scan the tasks folder once per hook run, and keep tempfile, shutil, hashlib, subprocess and other unused modules off the hot path, cutting PreToolUse Bash CPU from about 195 to 76 ms and PostToolUse Bash from 137 to 67 ms on real state (T-3577).
- The exam's planted hook wait now scales with machine load, so the self-change gate's loaded doubling rule still catches it and test_exam.LeanKindGateHookDelay stops failing merges at high load (T-3605)
- `sampo focus` and every task edit (set, ac, step, evidence) now hold the project lock only while they read and write the brief: git snapshots, recall, predict and the view regeneration run outside it, and an edit made meanwhile is kept (T-3558, T-3556).
- The close sentinel no longer starts a full check while a merge train's post-merge verify is pending or running, or once one has verified this code tree, so a batch gets one full suite run (T-3576).
- A full test-suite run holds one machine-wide slot (~/.cache/sampo/suite.lock), so a second full run waits its turn, saying every minute whose run it waits for, instead of three suites splitting the CPUs; targeted runs, nested runs and FOREMAN_NO_SUITE_SLOT=1 skip it (T-3541).
- The public .sampo mirror now names briefs and research notes without the home folder or a private term (cut short, or a host's own label): export writes a redacted name and removes the old-named copy, and import maps it back to this machine's own file (T-3574).
- A second live session in the same checkout is sent to its own lane and its main-checkout edits are refused with the lane path; liveness comes from the holder's process, SessionEnd frees it, and FOREMAN_NO_SESSION_LANES turns it off (T-3331).
- sampo ship no longer reruns the whole suite when main moved only data or docs past the train's gated tree (the tower's daily stats.json): it runs just the tests those files affect and reuses the gates' pass when they are green, while a plugin .py or .github difference still runs the suite (T-3533).
- With no task active and background work running, a Stop waits instead of pushing the next task, and a drive block that repeats the last one word for word is not said again until the work changes (T-3539).
- The public mirror no longer leaks a home path written after a $VAR, Claude's encoded project folder names, or the old content of mirror files left for the import, and no test names a real machine login (T-3547).
- sampo exam loop --run RUN [--model PROFILE] [--rounds N] answers each local profile's next unseen holdout scenarios on the smith arm and appends their failure classes to research/local-loop.jsonl (T-3484).
- Fleet: `next_id` reserves blocks of 32 T-numbers through refs/sampo/<slug>/ids by compare-and-swap push (needs FOREMAN_CLAIMS_REMOTE), prefetching the next block; hook paths never touch the network; offline past the block it refuses instead of minting (T-3329).
- Kalevala part 3: Väinämöinen watches every tower against what the user asked for and steers it back on track, in a loop that keeps going; its review fixes follow as T-3265 (T-3082).
- Fleet part 1: claims on git refs: one atomic push picks one winner, expired claims are taken over once, an unreadable holder is never taken, renewals batch every 10 minutes, offline says so, and the hostname is never written unasked (T-3321).
- A builder's first edit in a lane with no active task focuses the lane's own task (its foreman/T-N branch, or the one brief recording the lane) instead of a brief refusal, and the refusal that remains names the candidates or why focus refused (T-3519).
- The Sampo UI mod draws AskUserQuestion, command output, tool progress, notices and the session mode in Sampo's look; every drive push is one line of at most 160 characters, and the background-wait push comes at most once per task per 10 minutes, never repeated while the project lock is busy (T-3163).
- Headroom census (library): `sampo.headroom.census` lists a project's caps and fixed sets, and `raised` counts how often each cap went up in the last 180 days, cached by HEAD (T-3316).
- Merge trains run only the tests the batch's merged changes reach (plus the rule tests and the repo-wide scans) instead of the full suite, name a red lane's failing classes and the gate's last lines, and verify with the full suite in the background after landing; FOREMAN_FULL_GATE=1 restores the full gate (T-3544).
- sampo lane train checks and probes up to 3 lanes at once (1 when the host is over 2x its CPUs), keeps a serial run's left-out order, and skips a lane's affected-test run when its own green evidence ran on a tree whose diff to base plus lane touches none of the tests or the sources they cover (T-3531).
- sampo private add|rm|list keeps private host globs and words in Sampo's state only; the sync mirror shows them as <host> or <private> and rewrites itself when they change, and sampo ship and release --push refuse a push whose commits or tree carry one or a secrets-file value, naming files and commits, never the term, which never reaches a process's command line (T-3529).
- sampo task set depends_on= refuses an id that names no task ("unknown task T-9999") and the task's own id, and stores ids upper-cased; archived and closed tasks still count as valid dependencies (T-3420).
- The daily card publish refuses an export with empty or missing card files, empty kit demos, non-positive heights, more than 3 identical kit heights, or UI cards with no kit.json, and reports "cards refresh failed: <reason>" instead of committing it (T-3424).
- Lane merge's verify re-run and race checks use a load-scaled 1200 s base cap and report only a real timeout as one (a `timeout N` inside the verify reads as red); sampo task evidence --run records run seconds, and sampo task ac add|edit notes whole-module verifies whose last timed run took over 120 s (T-3418).
- A revert that a later re-land undid (citing 'This reverts commit <sha>') no longer spends risk points, and neither does the re-land: false post-merge reverts stop pausing autonomy (T-3492).
- A local-model bench runs beside sampo moons grow --keep on its own exam lock instead of the moons store lock, so a long grow no longer stops the nightly bench and the GPU keeps busy (T-3487).
- A close after a merge train no longer reruns its linked tests when the gates' full pass on the same code tree already ran them; it logs the pass it reused (T-3526).
- Test workers and louhi's exam children start with core dumps off, so crashing test binaries and SIGABRT-killed workers no longer leave dumps (about 8,500 a day, 4.1 GB) for systemd to compress (T-3503).
- sampo lane train leaves a lane out before any gate run when it conflicts with the base (git merge-tree) or its affected tests fail on the base plus that lane, twice and not on the bare base, naming the files or classes; a clean lane still lands in one gate run (T-3474).
- sampo sync export writes home folders as ~ and private or CGNAT hosts (user@ip, bare ip) as <host> in the public mirror, rewrites the files already mirrored on its next run, and its README says sampo sync (T-3508).
- sampo check, check --affected and the finish refusal print every failing class (at most 50 lines, then a count); run.py emits them all, the check_run event stores up to 200 with failed_n, and the evidence text puts the class names first (T-3397).
- The Rune page shows Kalevala: a city of towers beside Genesis with a key, a switch and a deep link; one bad tower no longer aborts the export (T-3266).
- sampo next and the drive name the finished lanes waiting on a merge with the last 2 h landing rate and the hours the pile takes to clear (T-3478).
- sampo ship reuses the gates' pass (a merge train's or a full sampo check's) when its test is the gates' own and the pushed tree differs from the gated one only in files its CI skips, so a ship after a train no longer reruns the whole suite (T-3468).
- Sampo's sentinel treats a re-verify that times out (run_command's "timed out after Ns", or run.py stopping at its deadline, which now exits 124) as inconclusive: no "Re-verify failed" FIX is filed, the entry stays queued for up to 3 timeouts and is then dropped with a notice, a rerun that times out no longer masks the first failure, and --json counts inconclusive (T-2944).
- last_change and first_touch date a Bash-sourced edit by its file mtime (data.at), floored at the Bash call's start and the task's creation, so an audit recorded between the edit and a late hook event no longer looks stale (T-3378).
- Kalevala: one tower's decide-and-act runs under its own per-tower lock with a 30 s deadline, so two concurrent steps snapshot a stalled tower and log each act once, and a busy tower is skipped instead of hanging (T-3332).
- Hook timing events (`hook_ms`) from PreToolUse record the tool name and the guard's own decision time (`guard_ms`, plus `decided` when it asked or denied), so a slow handler can be pinned to the guard or to the notes after it (T-3352).
- Self-checks in sampo next and the drive, in every state: landed commits unshipped 2+ h after the last sampo ship, a retired mirror folder tracked beside the current one, and the user's asks from the last 3 days not landed, oldest first (T-3127).
- Two or more finished lanes are offered as one sampo lane train (one gate run, a red set bisected) instead of one sampo lane merge each; sampo which and capture's done-work note now find the train (T-3467).
- sampo lane merge records each criterion pass on the lane's clean tip tree and reuses it while that tree is unchanged, and sampo lane precheck ID runs a lane's criteria ahead of its merge, so a queue runner checks the next lanes in parallel while one merge lands (T-3444).
- A child sampo (an exam's Smith under a temp home) logs its model calls to the parent's call log again: the path lives under FOREMAN_CALLS, the name core.mirror_env leaves (T-3448).
- Re-landed T-3377 (scope+=PATH): a post-merge runner at a pre-T-3442 sha reverted it on the slot-pool false reds (T-3454).
- The post-merge suite settles pending merges with one run on main's HEAD even after a revert: requeued merges no longer fall back to one oldest-first run each on old trees (T-3456).
- A task's close no longer counts another task's merged-in doc (named in its Docs impact) or a revert of another task's merge as its own out-of-scope edit (T-3452).
- The post-merge suite runs once on the newest pending merge first: green there verifies every older pending merge in its history, so one 20-40 min run covers a night of merges instead of one run each; a red newest merge still finds the culprit oldest first (T-3446).
- T-3120 is back: sampo task finish reuses a full-gates pass on the same tree when the only change since is CHANGELOG.md lines. The post-merge suite had reverted it on CheckSkip, Claims and CloseGates reds that the slot-pool bug (T-3442) caused (T-3450).
- T-2911 (an automatic criterion run checks off only its own step) and T-3372 (sampo task ac list and rm verbs; a lone verb-like word is refused) are back: the post-merge suite had reverted both on CheckSkip and Claims reds that the slot-pool bug (T-3442) caused, red at their parents too (T-3445).
- A run.py nested in a test class no longer queues on the machine-wide CPU slot pool: importing sampo renames SAMPO_SLOT to FOREMAN_SLOT, which run.py never read, so fixtures' nested fm check and evidence runs waited behind the ship and the post-merge suite (CheckSkip 323-344 s with timeouts, 4 s now). The false reds this made auto-reverted merges and kept refusing ships (T-3442).
- run.py --load N tops the host up to at most twice its CPU count, counting the busy loops other runs already hold, and says when it cuts the number: three --load 32 runs on 16 CPUs held 96 loops and load 100 for 12 minutes (T-3441).
- sampo ship no longer refuses when the live tower pushes moons-catalogue shards mid-test: test.yml skips the shards as data (no test reads the committed ones), and the moved-while-testing check reads the CI globs of the tree being pushed, the workflow GitHub runs, not the moved main's (T-3439).
- Kalevala watch shows a tower whose files can't be read as its own "unreadable" row with the reason instead of blanking the whole block, a round lists it as skipped, and the genesis drawer's close keeps the page's query string (T-3376).
- Task boundaries file the predicted next asks that no open task covers as predicted captures (at most 3 open project-wide, no refiling within 7 days, guesses of 3+ words), and sampo misses counts a catch when the user later asks for one (T-3128).
- An edit made while sampo task finish runs is noted ("a close of ID is running: this edit isn't part of it") and journalled to no task, only in the closing tree: a close in the main checkout leaves lane builders' edits attributed, and concurrent closes each keep their own marker (T-3030).
- The next-request predictor counts as leading only when a paired sign test over the replay gives p < 0.05 with at least a 2-point lead, so 259 against 256 of 376 reads "level with naive"; trailing and no-baseline kinds say so, and the self-running line shows the counts and p-value (T-3069).
- moons grow --keep waits out a busy or slow local model (30/60/120/240 s, about 7.5 min in all, clearing the down-window before each retry) and retries before it stops with "stopped: local after waiting N s" (T-3096).
- sampo task prove accepts a FIX whose regression test already existed: with no changed test file it runs the check on the start tree as it was and now, so red then green is proved when the red is a real test failure, not an import error or a missing script (T-3107).
- The site build test proves the published share image is a real PNG file (over 10 KB, PNG signature) and that no published asset is a symlink (T-3059).
- Rune's drift check no longer flags a seed line on one noisy cold read: a flagged line gets a second read and counts as drifted only when both name it, a line only one read names is listed as unconfirmed, drift.md records both reads' verdicts, and a clean first read still costs one call (T-3056).
- The Stop hook holds a reply once when its shell code block has an irreversible step (rm -r, mv -f or into an absolute or ~ path, dd, mkfs, git reset --hard, git clean, forced push, truncating redirect, SQL DROP) without set -euo pipefail at its top or a check before the step, unless the session already ran that line; it names the line and the script shape (T-3001).
- sampo queue, tidy and the status "Dangling deps" line no longer flag a dependency on an archived (closed) task; archived ids are read from the archive's filenames and count as done (T-3430).
- sampo ship reruns red test classes alone after a load wait and refuses only on those still red alone, in both the affected-first and full-suite steps; flakes are named and file no baseline task (T-3435).
- The post-merge suite no longer reverts a prose-only merge (MASTER.md, README, CHANGELOG, docs/, the state mirror) for a red test class that is still red alone after a load wait but whose source names none of the changed files; code merges, other .md files (skills, commands, agents, rules), crashes and import errors are blamed as before, and the revert note names "reached by: <files>" (T-3425).
- sampo task block and defer keep a task's file leases again (T-3398 had them released, breaking T-2870's rule that open tasks keep theirs); leases are released only when a task is done or dropped (T-3432).
- roundtrip.py clones without --branch when HEAD is detached, so sampo lane train's scratch tree no longer fails the e2e base check as "Remote branch HEAD not found" and trains can land (T-3429).
- sampo doctor warns, in a Sampo checkout only, naming the fm*.py old-import shims nothing imports any more, once the plugin reaches 1.4.0 (core.ALIAS_UNTIL, two minors after the rename) (T-3095).
- Doctor shows each lane class's subagent effort (builder, reviewer, scout), notes when CLAUDE_CODE_SUBAGENT_EFFORT (Claude Code 2.1.316) overrides them, and gates builder worktree reliance on Claude Code 2.1.311 (T-3384).
- sampo task set takes scope+=PATH[,PATH…] and scope-=PATH (also depends_on) to widen or trim a list without re-listing it, resolved under the task lock so concurrent widenings keep each other's paths (T-3377).
- plugin/tests/run.py accepts test file paths (plugin/tests/test_X.py, ./…, test_X.py, an absolute path into the tests dir) as the module name test_X; any other path is a clear error (T-3428).
- sampo task finish and sampo check reuse a passing full-gates run from any task on the same code tree (CHANGELOG.md and the mirror's .md files aside; a changed CHANGELOG still gets its own docs check), printing "gates: reusing the full run … on the same code tree" with the run's date, tree and task; the red-gate refusal and the post-close sentinel honour it too (T-3120).
- sampo task ac ID list prints the criteria and ac ID rm N removes one (refused when it or a later criterion has evidence); a lone lowercase word after ac ID is refused as a mistyped verb instead of becoming a criterion (T-3372).
- An automatic test run no longer checks off the task's current step because it shares a runner with a verify command; only the step's or a criterion's verify command, or a project gate command, does (normalised for a leading cd, a trailing tail/head/grep and spacing) (T-2911).
- rune.step and rune.towers share one ledger scan per ledger state (keyed by each file's path, mtime, size and inode) instead of one per tower: the 27-tower step loop drops from 3.05 s to 0.27 s with byte-identical output (T-3375).
- Closing a task (sampo task finish, done, drop, block, defer) releases its file leases at once instead of holding hub files from other lanes for about 20 minutes; the release touches only that project's leases, and a blocked or deferred lane keeps its pre-claim (T-3398).
- core.log_event caps list-valued data fields at 200 (the true count in <field>_n, a caller's own count kept) and strings at 4000 characters before hashing, so one event can't bloat the hash-chained ledger; verify-ledger --accept-damage acks in chunks of 200 so none is cut (T-3403).
- Re-landed T-3410 (the fmtrain bullet in MASTER.md and its map entry) after the post-merge suite reverted it on load-only failures; test_docs passes again (T-3426).
- test_cli.AffectedPairs runs in about 3 s instead of 90-240 s: its nested lane repo's tests/run.py is a small stand-in that runs the named modules and fails a skipped or missing name, with every assertion kept (T-3417).
- Six timing tests no longer depend on the machine: the stall test sets the mtime ahead, the doctor test stubs the CPU count (closes T-3308), the demo test drops its wall-clock assertion, and the docs, CLI and showpiece tests scale or retry under load (T-3367).
- Stored verifies written as `python3 -m unittest test_X.Class` now run (at lane merge, finish, re-verify and sentinel) as `python3 plugin/tests/run.py test_X.Class`, or from the folder holding the test when the repo has no run.py; `sampo task ac add|edit --verify` prints a note when it does, and the lane merge refusal names the criterion command and its first ERROR line (T-3411).
- doctor: the statusline and rules-symlink checks WARN instead of FAIL when run from a linked git worktree of the installed checkout (wired at main, running in a worktree; the reverse still FAILs), so `sampo lane train` gates can pass; git calls time out to FAIL and statusLine commands with arguments are parsed (T-3406).
- MASTER.md and the docs/map shard list the fmtrain shim (T-3405's old import path, kept for two releases), so test_docs.MapShards and test_docs.PluginFiles pass again (T-3410).
- Hook time limits stretch under test load through one clamped FOREMAN_HOOK_SLACK factor (1-10, tests only), and slow fm task new/focus calls in hooks time out cleanly instead of blocking an edit (T-3366).
- Test runner: each class's timer scales with machine load but never outlasts the run's deadline (the gate passes its own as FM_RUN_DEADLINE), and a class killed by its timer is recorded as slow so the next run splits it (T-3338).
- `sampo check --affected` picks tests for a hub file such as cli.py by the symbols that changed, closed over in-file use and command verbs, and runs the whole file's tests when it can't tell (T-3306).
- sampo.train has its fmtrain import shim, like every other module (T-3405).
- test_cli.AffectedPairs's nested fm check --affected runs get a 300 s base hang guard; 120 s still ran out inside a close's own gate run (T-3404).
- `sampo lane train T-1,T-2` merges the named lanes in a scratch worktree, re-runs each lane's criteria, gates once, bisects a red set to the culprit lanes and fast-forwards the branch; `sampo check` reuses the pass while only CHANGELOG.md and the sync mirror change (T-3380).
- fm sync's repo mirror is now .sampo/ (was .foreman/): one constant names it, an older .foreman/ is read until it moves once in a single logged commit (git mv), the move runs only from the checkout you stand in (a lane never moves another checkout), a user's own .sampo/ is left alone, and the guard and lint cover both names (T-3342).
- The fmrepro import shim matches the other shims exactly, so test_rename_aliases is green again after the repro merge (T-3400).
- test_cli.AffectedPairs gives its nested `fm check --affected` runs a 120 s base hang guard, so a loaded host stops failing every close on them (T-3390).
- **Reproducible build check.** `sampo repro` builds the site and README figures twice and from REF, and names the nondeterministic and changed files, removals included (T-3279).
- `sampo sync` is cheap again: an export walks only the folders that travel and skips every file whose source and mirror copy are unchanged since the last export (a size-and-mtime stamp per file), so an unchanged export takes ~0.3-0.5 s instead of 6-8 s under the project lock on every state write (T-3349).
- A builder's `sampo lease take` is no longer refused by another lane's brief pre-claim (pre-claims stay advisory, with a one-line note naming them); an explicit lease of another session still refuses (T-3283).
- `sampo sync on` works on this repo's state: atomic writes cut the file name in their temp name (a 250-byte brainstorm file refused the mirror), and the commit scan no longer takes a placeholder URL password (`user:pass@host`) or an ISO date under a secret-like name (`token_expires = "2030-01-01"`) for a credential (T-3336).
- Test runs share the machine: every `run.py` draws its classes from one slot pool sized to the CPUs (or the cgroup's quota), so ten runs at once queue for 16 slots instead of starting 160 workers; the tests' `fm()` calls go through a fork server that imports Sampo once (2.4x cheaper per call); `perf_bench.py` records suite and CLI timings with host load; `sampo lane max` goes up to 100 builders (T-3297, part 1 of T-3252).
- A builder's edit inside its own lane worktree counts for the lane's task, never for the main checkout's active task, so a close no longer fails its scope gate while builders work (T-3256).
- The live page's modern look draws every panel with one small chart kit (no library, under the strict CSP): calls per minute by model and spend over the day as time series, GPU memory stacked per model, the moons funnel, sparklines, running jobs' progress bars with percent, rate and ETA, and steps done of total for tasks and lanes; each chart has a text equivalent, and the network canvas turns light in the light scheme (T-3092).
- The README draws every table and four charts as pictures in the live page's modern look, light and dark: `python3 demo/readme_figures.py` redraws them from the README's own rows (kept under each picture as text) and from measured data (tasks closed a day and tests over time from git, hook time from bench_hooks.py, the model bench), `--check` names a stale one, and `sampo works check --type figure` gates a document figure at its shown size; the contrast gate no longer reads a one-level raster difference between its two renders as faint text (T-3247).
- `sampo sandbox` binds back, read-only, what a job reads from under /tmp or /run and its working folder there, so `run.py --sandbox` works from a repo under /tmp, as in `sampo ship`'s scratch tree (T-3246).
- The live page's jobs list reads a python3 command's program past options with a value (`-X dev`, `-W ignore`), follows `-m MODULE`, and names no program for `-c CODE`, so those runs are no longer missed (T-3199).
- The first builder lane is never starved by moderate load: with no builder working, `sampo lane brief` starts one unless the load is past 3 per CPU; further lanes keep the 1.5 per CPU bar. Sampo's own test sweeps and fills had held the load over the bar for three hours with nothing being built (T-3243).
- The hook-imports bench runs its scratch project with background work off and tolerates a late writer when cleaning up, so its teardown no longer fails with 'Directory not empty' on CI (T-3241).
- The live-jobs test that reads a real process's start time skips where there is no /proc (macOS), as the live page itself reads processes only through /proc (T-3240).
- The calling-card tests skip where a card's call can't be confined (macOS has no Landlock), as the confinement tests already did, so CI on macOS isn't red for a refusal that is by design (T-3238).
- `sampo works check` and the UI card examples read the contrast of every text colour on every background, not just the commonest pairs, so a small badge or one faint line under 4.5:1 fails; antialiasing shades and hinting variants still don't count. It caught the UI kit's form controls on the browser's own dark-theme grey (4.34:1); they now take the kit's surface colour (T-3226).
- The test runner gives every test class `FOREMAN_NO_LOCAL_MODELS=1` unless you set it yourself, so a test that leans on this machine's Ollama models fails here the way it would on CI, which has none (T-3229).
- UI cards show a rendered example: a local model writes one per card, kept only when its render check passes (not blank, fits 390 px, 4.5:1 text in both themes, under 12 KB, nothing to sanitise), else the card shows its component's hand-written kit demo, in a sandboxed, script-free frame that follows the page theme (T-3102).
- Guard: printf's `-v` counts only before its format, so a `command -v` inside a substitution among printf's arguments, beside an unquoted `$t`, no longer blocks a loop as "IFS may be changed"; a first word an expansion, brace or backtick could turn into `-v` is now refused as a computed name (T-3223).
- The moons tests pin their embedding model instead of finding this box's Ollama ones, so they pass on CI runners with no local models (main's CI was red on Families and Promote since 2026-11-04) (T-3220).
- Post-merge verify keeps a merge as inherited only when the commit before it names each of the same test classes failing; a class the merge added (the parent finds no such test) or a parent run that crashes leaves the red the merge's own, so it is reverted (T-3224).
- The post-merge check no longer reverts a merge for a red its first parent already has: the record says inherited with the classes, and only the merge that brought the red is reverted (T-3213).
- `sampo works check` counts the copies nested patterns, markers, masks, clips and filters make, through url(#id) in attributes, styles and stylesheet rules, toward its 5,000-copy cap, so an exponential pattern or marker bomb fails `xml` like a `<use>` bomb does; more than 64 stylesheet rules naming a url(#id) fail it too (T-3198).
- The crest's Sampo mark is hand-drawn pixel art in four flat tones (fire, steel, stone) instead of a blurry downscale, and shot.py draws half blocks exactly (T-3210).
- Calling cards: `sampo card list|show|call|test|rank|bench` runs verified, confined generators a small model calls by name, with seed cards, an embedding ranker offered through fm code, and a paired with-versus-without measurement (T-3160).
- Kalevala part 2: `sampo kalevala start`, `step`, `gen` and `select` run each aspect's Rune tower as a sealed job, at most K at once and charged once inside Kalevala's own daily share, keep Kalevala's own generations, and carry closed behaviour changes into every tower's next seed as capabilities (T-3081).
- Guard: a python heredoc followed by `2>&1` and read-only filter pipes (head, tail without follow, cut, wc, sort without -o/-T, flag-only uniq, grep with literal args) is judged as python, not shell text, unless the shell text could make a filter's name run something else (T-2261).
- Moons yield more and can be about Sampo itself: a payoff line missing only "With this," is fixed at write and 847 old rejects are re-admitted; a Sampo grid (problem solving, memory, efficiency and every coverage area) is read thinnest first beside the user grid and accepts Sampo's own parts as the payoff; a moon whose kill test needs more than one person and one day gets one rewritten test as a child row; each cell's prompt carries its kind's top reject reasons and two kept moons (T-3045).
- Direction 12 (the illustrated forge) is kept in the repo: its page, notes, `_src` generator and logos under `design/site/12-forge-illustrated/`, and the port scripts that made the landing site under `site/tools/`, which now write an output only when its content changes (T-3097).
- More builders at once: a lane whose builder has returned and only waits on a merge no longer holds a builder slot (up to 20 may wait unmerged), and `sampo lane max` goes up to 16 (T-3135).
- A builder's own stop now frees its slot: SubagentStop marks its lane returned, so lanes launched from a session that is still running stop counting against the builder cap; SubagentStart clears the mark (T-3145).
- A builder whose worktree starts ahead of its brief's base (main moved on after the brief was written) builds on it and says so, instead of stopping (T-3142).
- A builder lane that has returned (its own stop mark, or commits ahead with only untracked leftovers such as node_modules) or already merged is no longer called STALLED with advice to stop and recover it (T-3147).
- The moons fill no longer stalls silently on a failing local model: each call tries every usable local model in turn (Jarvis, then this machine's GPU models), skips a failed one for 10 minutes instead of waiting out its 300 s timeout every call, and a local-only stop names each model tried and its error (T-3152).
- The hook tests' no-task gate runs as six parallel classes instead of one: its 31 tests went from 57.9 s to 12.8 s (T-3151).
- Tests can no longer write to the real repo: the test helpers drop an inherited GIT_DIR (and the other variables that redirect git) at import, after a run wrote core.bare=true and the fixture identity into Sampo's own .git/config and every merge failed; fm doctor now fails a working tree marked bare (T-3185).
- Fast merges part 1: the hook bench records which modules each hook loads at runtime, so a change outside them can skip the self-change gate's bench (T-3139).
- Targeted runs never wait for load: task evidence, check --affected and a finish's criterion checks start at once with a load-scaled timeout; a full check or lane merge waits at most 60 s (T-3144).
- Back-to-back merges onto one main bench the base tree once: the self-change gate caches the base side's bench and guard replay by the base tree's content hash, signed with a key only the user's account can read, and reuses the bench only at a similar machine load (T-3176).
- Ward treats every file under `~/.config/sampo` as a credential (secrets.env, selfgate.key and any later key), not only by file name, with a replay case for each (T-3188).
- Smith no longer lets a model quit early: a done with no edit made while the plan's verify command fails gets that failure back as the next turn, at most twice, and early-done stops are counted per model (T-3180).
- Briefs, criteria templates and playbooks name `claude plugin test mods/foreman-ui` for the UI mod, never `npx vitest`, which can't run in a fresh lane and refused a merge (T-3143).
- `sampo lane brief` refuses a task whose own commits are already in main and says to re-run its criteria and finish it, so a merged task never gets a second builder; a revert reopens it (T-3168).
- `sampo works check` renders each SVG design headless and holds it to its type's gates with perceptual dedupe, and survives hostile SVGs: endless colours, tiny viewBoxes, `<use>` bombs, megabyte regex traps, hung renders and crashing files each fail only their own work, and the browser runs with no secrets and no network (T-3106).
- SAMPO_ settings reach child processes: SAMPO_JEFF_HOME, SAMPO_SECRETS and SAMPO_CARDS are read under both names, so a child Sampo launches sees the folder you set (T-3173).
- The live page's modern look is the default and reads clean: the charts no longer overlap the deck below 1200 px, the network's lines and labels hold their contrast in the light scheme, buttons are 28 px tall with a red outline for pause, no forge warmth leaks into the modern look, and the forge stays one toggle away (T-3054).
- Your standing yes now passes self-change gate holds that are only about footprint, verb count or hook speed, so those merges no longer stop to ask you; a change that would make the guard block less still waits for your own yes (T-3219).
- The self-change gate's cache key is always the account's own `~/.config/sampo/selfgate.key`, never a path SAMPO_SECRETS or FOREMAN_SECRETS points elsewhere, so a run with those redirected can't verify or forge a cached base bench (T-3192).
- The post-merge check re-runs a red test class alone before reverting: a class that fails only inside the full suite but passes alone is logged as flaky and the merge stays; one that also fails alone still reverts exactly that merge (T-3216).
- The guard's self-match check judges each alternative of a process-search pattern, so `pgrep -af "[t]2878|[s]mith"` passes while one unbracketed alternative still refuses, and a pgrep only quoted inside another command's argument is no longer refused (T-3154).
- A miss ledger: each message where you point out something Sampo should have caught is logged with its class (stalled, unfiled, unmeasured, unused, invisible, unreachable, dead, other), and `sampo misses` counts them by class and per day (T-3126).
- `sampo task new` and `sampo task ac` warn when a criterion's verify command can't run in a fresh lane: a `cd` into a folder then npx/npm/yarn with no committed package.json or lockfile there, a run.py target that matches no test, or a program not on PATH (T-3189).
- The live page shows each running job's progress the whole run: training (step of total, loss, ETA), small cards, the moons funnel, exams and genesis; a keep run carries "kept N/goal", repairs get their own bar, task rows and lane benches show steps and stage with rate and ETA in both themes, and a command that only mentions jeff-train is not taken for a training job (T-3044).
- Merges land after their fast checks: the full suite then runs on main in the background, a red result reverts exactly that merge and reopens its lane with the failing tests named, and further merges wait while a landed merge is red (T-3141).
- The Sampo pane opens with a crest: the Sampo mark ringed by runes that light in turn while work runs, and a hammer per running subagent in its own colour, striking with sparks; under 60 columns it folds to a 3-row mark, and the Clawd mascot is still an option (T-3113).
- `sampo doctor` warns when Ollama loads a local model with less context than models.json declares for it, naming the profile and both numbers (T-3164).
- The live page counts only builders still working: "N at work · M waiting to merge", with waiting lanes dimmed in the network view (T-3170).
- Moons yield: a medium-tier moon gets one "go wilder" rewrite judged again, a cell's write prompt carries up to two of its kept high-tier moons, `sampo genesis moonshot bench-writers` measures kept per call per writer, and the live page projects when 10,000 kept lands; the fill takes only the bench's writer, never a laxer judge (T-3122).
- `sampo exam run` and each `sampo genesis` step report their progress (scenarios or items done of total), so the live page shows a bar for them (T-3086).
- Kalevala part 1: `sampo genesis --tower NAME` gives each Rune tower its own home under one project, while the moons store, exam scenarios and your steers stay shared at the root; `sampo genesis towers` lists each tower's aspect, generation and last verdicts (T-3053).
- The self-change gate's planted-growth test runs as one test per growth over shared benches: its slowest part takes 51 s instead of 238-390 s, so it no longer sets the full suite's floor (T-3171).
- A close that spans another lane's merge counts audits made both before and after the merge; an adversary audit recorded after the merge no longer reads as stale (T-3200).
- `sampo digest` and `sampo doctor`'s usage-burn scan parse only transcript lines that carry usage: 2.3x less CPU over a month of transcripts (22 s to 9.6 s here), and the digest clock test no longer reads your real `~/.claude` (T-3193).
- The site has a Cards page: every small-model card, searchable by title, when line and command, filtered by grid and cell with counts, opened in place with a #K-id permalink, in light and dark and on phones; the daily publish re-exports it (T-3101).
- Test runs split a big class into one job per test, longest first from the last run's times, keeping classes with class- or module-level setup whole; failures still name each test (T-3166).
- A verb that fails with a plain ValueError says so and how to capture it, instead of calling the state corrupt and sending you to `sampo fsck`; files that don't parse still say corrupt (T-3194).
- A full `sampo check`'s suite gate gets at least 30 min, scaled by load per CPU and capped at 2 h, and a gate that times out says the load and deadline it had (T-3174).
- Job progress, the data half: Sampo's long loops write a per-pid progress file and the live page's jobs show done, total, rate and ETA (T-3083).
- The site's Benchmarks page has a Models section: a $0 rerun of the model bench every Rune generation and the promotion rule with its decision rows (T-3093).
- The live page has a Network view beside the Forge: agents, tools and jobs, and models drawn as a net, with a bead travelling each new call (T-3034).
- `sampo task new --from` no longer overwrites a planned task that has evidence (T-3087); `sampo task drop` refuses an active or verifying task unless --force (T-3088); `sampo code -p` help says it makes a task and points to --ask and --dry-plan for a one-off probe (T-3089).
- `sampo which` finds the task set key forms ('widen task scope', 'approve a task', 'grant a guard category') with their exact commands; built end to end by the local 27B model through Smith (T-3108).
- Every kept moon is used: export writes all survivors to a catalogue beside genesis.json, the site's Moons page lists them all with search, grid, tier and family filters and paging, and promotion draws from the whole catalogue, family leads first, each draw recorded so none repeats (T-3047).
